### Shadow-depth root cause: проверка ветки `fix/darkness-parity` — 2026-10-25

- По сообщению Claude найден root cause остаточной темноты: при shadow projection shader pack записывает GL clip-space depth, который Vulkan shadow pass трактует как zero-to-one. Probe показал сохранённый `shadowtex1` depth `0.2*z_GL` вместо значения, ожидаемого pack comparison (`0.1*z_GL+0.5`); в terrain `lmCoord` совпадает с Iris в пределах 1–2/255, но `shadowMult=0` на освещённых поверхностях против примерно `0.56` у Iris. В `composite5` до tonemap небо совпадает, трава у Veyra примерно в 10 раз темнее.
- Предлагаемая правка remap-ит `gl_Position.z` в shadow vertex stages после pack main: `(z + w) * 0.5`; entity bridge также направляет shadow projection macros на `shadowProjection`. Это соответствует переходу из GL clip depth `[-w,w]` к Vulkan `[0,w]`, но интегрированный результат пока не проверен независимо.
- Claude сообщил, что terrain-only GPU probe поднял средний RGB Veyra с `58/59/53` до `103/94/68` при Iris `122/109/81`, а MAD снизился `64 → 24`; это сообщение из thread, не наш повторный GPU прогон.
- Read-only проверка текущего worktree `veyra-darkness`: ветка `fix/darkness-parity` перебазирована на `a2adf97`; изменены `TerrainVertexShaderAdapter`, `EntityVertexShaderAdapter` и два соответствующих unit-test файла. Свежие локальные JUnit XML (2026-10-25 03:33:47): 76 suites / 591 tests / 0 failures / 0 errors / 0 skipped. Это проверяет adapter logic, но не заменяет итоговый GPU lifecycle на rebased terrain+entity реализации.
- На момент проверки Minecraft/Gradle test процесса не было; локальные `logs/` и `run-cmp/` не трогал. В Claude thread последним сообщением зафиксирован лимит сессии; PR и точные hosted checks для этой правки ещё не проверены. Не редактировал shadow projection/vertex adapters по ранее переданному ограничению.
- Beta.47 с #127/#128 уже опубликован и его metadata слита в `main` (`a2adf97`). Новую release ветку или публикацию не создавал; следующий cut оставляю после завершения review, GPU проверки и CI для shadow-depth fix.

### Read-only review entity shadow WIP — 2026-10-25

- В dirty diff `fix/darkness-parity` заметил риск: `EntityVertexShaderAdapter.prepare()` заранее раскрывает pack `ftransform()` и `gl_ProjectionMatrix` в `ProjMat`, а изменение `bridge.replace("ProjMat", "shadowProjection")` выполняется позже и меняет только native bridge, не подготовленный pack source. Текущий тест проверяет bridge-макрос, но не выражение, полученное из реального pack `main()`, где Complementary использует `ftransform()` для вычисления shadow-space `position`.

### Shadow-depth darkness root cause; beta.47/beta.48 published - 2026-10-25

- Capture paths ruled out by Codex (raw Vulkan == Minecraft Screenshot, byte-identical). Bisected with matched Veyra/Iris pack probes: pre-tonemap grass ~10x darker; lmCoord matched Iris; shadowMult was 0 on all lit surfaces (Iris ~0.56); stored shadow depth == 0.2*z_GL exactly vs pack compare 0.1*z_GL+0.5. Cause: pack shadow stages write OpenGL clip depth via Iris shadowProjection (#124) into Veyra's zero-to-one shadow pass without the GL window remap -> everything shadowed.
- Fix: gl_Position.z = (z + w) * 0.5 after pack shadow mains (terrain + entity), entity shadow projection uses shadowProjection. Terrain-only result: mean RGB 103/94/68 vs Iris 122/109/81 (before 58/59/53), MAD 64->24; Codex integrated run MAE 48.6->20.4. Landed on main as 604562d (includes Codex's ProjMat-in-pack-source correction); my duplicate local branch was dropped.
- Beta.47 (27d91c0: #127 + #128) and beta.48 (120cc8f: + shadow-depth fix, block-entity text BlockTrans, deferred Vulkan resource flush #130) were published by the Codex thread. Independently verified beta.48 downloaded assets: SHA256SUMS match, verify_jar.py accepted.
- Remaining: residual ~15-level sky/wall gap vs Iris, dynamic entity-shadow GPU check, natural-world visuals, Sildur sign text (Codex investigating), stable parity not claimed.

### 2026-10-26 - Sildur sign-text clip-space probe (beta.48)

- Re-ran the pinned Sildur v2.02 Extreme GraphicsSmoke fixture on the RTX 3070 in the beta.48 worktree. Each run completed with BUILD SUCCESSFUL and result.json status=passed; this is only the general smoke/lifecycle gate, not a glyph visibility assertion.
- The scene sign text draw reaches minecraft:pipeline/text_polygon_offset with 156 vertices and is routed to gbuffers_block_translucent on MRT colortex4. The captured native clip bounds are x=-0.0264..0.0285, y=-0.1875..-0.1536, z=0.993687..0.993693.
- Diagnostic shader changes show that the pack-generated clip is still wrong: an opaque magenta fragment with the normal pack clip produces no glyph pixels; substituting native X/Y/Z/W and moving Z forward by 0.001*W produces a thin magenta line; 0.00001*W does not; retaining pack X/Y while substituting native Z/W still produces no line. This points to a pack vertex clip-space X/Y mismatch, with depth ordering also contributing. The exact matrix/input cause is not established.
- The sign-text pipeline's UBO 0 is marked global=true, so the missing useGlobalBuffer flag fixed by #127 is not the issue. Supplier maps report nonzero gbufferModelView and gbufferModelViewInverse, but the captured probes do not prove the final GPU matrix values.
- Temporary adapter and GraphicsSmoke mixin changes were restored; no production source, PR, merge, or release changed. Diagnostic captures and logs are preserved in C:\Users\Gleb\.t3\scratch\veyra-beta48-release-20261025\build\reports\sildur-signtext-*20261026 and the corresponding run-graphics-smoke work directory.
- Next validation must compare the original pack clip coordinates with native clip at the actual glyph vertices, then assert readable glyph shape and transparent gaps in the sign ROI against a matched Iris+Sildur capture. Do not treat the general smoke pass or the magenta probes as a fix.

### Sky and wall parity vs Iris (PRs #134, #135) - 2026-10-25

- Region analysis on beta.48 (Complementary HIGH vs Iris 1.10.8, same fixture): ground ~1.0, sky ~0.75, wall ~0.77.
- Sky: #127 was incomplete - VulkanMod's parser creates "UBO 0" fields via Uniform.createUniformInfo without Uniform.Info.setupSupplier(), and Vec1f/Vec1i.update skip null suppliers, so loose uniforms still read 0. Self-calibrating skybasic probe sunAngle*10: 1/255 -> 95/255 (Iris 93). PR #134 (b75b36f) attaches suppliers after precompile; sky ratio ~1.08. Codex re-tested Sildur sign text on #134: lifecycle passes, sign text still not readable (Codex lane).
- Wall: probes showed lmCoord, shadowMult and world normal identical to Iris; terrain output ~0.75x. Cause: vanilla directional face shading baked into chunk vertex colours; Iris disables it unless oldLighting=true. PR #135 (17c2d25): readOldLighting, ClientLevelShadeMixin (ClientLevel.getShade -> UP), terrain rebuild on change. Wall ratio -> 0.94-0.98. 596 tests.
- Release beta.49 held by agreement with Codex until Sildur sign text and my wall/shadow work are settled. Next: dynamic entity-shadow GPU fixture.

### Dynamic entityColor overlay diagnosis and fix review - 2026-10-25

- Claude's golem probe wrote `entityColor.rgb * 0.5 + entityColor.a * 0.5` to a diagnostic target; the unhurt iron golem sampled as white at multiple pixels despite its correct albedo. Source comparison with Iris `EntityPatcher` confirms the convention mismatch: Vulkan/vanilla `overlayColor.a=1` means no overlay, while Iris exposes `entityColor.a=1-overlayColor.a` and clears RGB when that result is zero. This explains the constant washed-out lighting and also matches the earlier shadow-alpha symptom as a plausible shared cause; the post-fix shadow still needs GPU confirmation.

### Sign text depth-path source comparison - 2026-10-25

- Read the generated `sign-text-gbuffers_block_translucent-pipeline-text_polygon_offset.vsh` from the exact-main swapped-Z fixture. The Sildur vertex main derives `viewPos` through `ModelViewMat`, reconstructs `position` with `gbufferModelViewInverse`, then applies `gbufferModelView` and `ProjMat` for `gl_Position`. Veyra captures `gbufferModelView` and its inverse from the same current model-view matrix and uses the native projection, so this chain is mathematically equivalent to native clip position; it does add floating-point operations.
- Inference only: accumulated rounding could move glyph fragments across the sign depth boundary, consistent with the earlier Z-only survival difference and small-bias probes. It is not yet proven; next probe should record pack-versus-native clip Z for the same glyph vertices/fragments and test that delta against the copied pipeline's depth bias. Do not add a global text depth bias from the current sparse counts.

### Dynamic shadow fade and viewport probes - 2026-10-25

- Claude's Veyra-only fixture disabled Complementary's `if (opacity < dither) discard;` entity shadow distance-fade condition. GraphicsSmoke ran successfully, but the matched ground ratio remained about 1.14, so that fade discard does not explain the absent golem shadow.

During Veyra entity-shadow features, `VkCommandEncoderMixin` supplies `VeyraShadowRenderer.shadowColorTextureView()`, backed by the square shadow target allocated at `shadowSettings.resolution()`. Draw logs also identify the shadow MRT framebuffer. A stale window-sized viewport is therefore unlikely.
- Claude is running a temporary magenta entity marker into `shadowcolor0` and displaying that target through `composite5` to locate shadow-pass writes. ## Shadow entity depth diagnostic - 2026-10-25

One agreed GPU run completed on a clean diagnostic branch from `6e0d7ba`; no production source was changed. The temporary entity-only near-depth shader override was confirmed as the final pack assignment before Veyra's depth remap. A terrain probe sampled 110,875 screen pixels; none observed zero depth in the intended UV region. The player's expected footprint was within that region and read far depth, while the golem footprint was clamped to the region edge and partly obscured by its screen-space body. This supports (but does not conclusively prove, because player shadow submission was assumed) that shadow-entity fragments did not write depth at the forced texels. No further GPU run is authorized or planned. The diagnostic branch, run-dir pack edits, and temporary summon changes were removed/restored; the diagnostic worktree is detached at `6e0d7ba` with no tracked changes. Entity-shadow depth remains an unresolved release limitation.

### Sildur sign-text source re-audit - 2026-10-25

- Re-read current beta.49 candidate sources on PR #137's code base. `ShaderPack.blockTextProgram(true)` selects `gbuffers_block_translucent` for block-entity text even when the ordinary block-translucent program is disabled; `readBlockTextFragment` and `compileSignTextPipelines` load its fragment and vertex stages. `TextSubmissionContexts` carries the sign's block identifier across deferred text submission/rendering, and `EntityShaderAdapter` wraps text fragments with the native alpha cutoff.
- Unit coverage exists for disabled `BlockTrans` selection, identity-keyed deferred text context/nesting, and alpha discard insertion. This establishes source routing and adapter behavior, not visible Sildur glyphs.
- Existing saved Sildur captures do not provide a clean current-head scene-pass glyph assertion; the trace must exclude shadow rendering and inspect the sign's text ROI. No GPU run was made in this audit. Sign readability remains unverified; entity shadow depth remains unresolved.

### Runtime dump shadow-entity GLSL — 2026-10-26

- Повторный RTX 3070 GraphicsSmoke с `-PgraphicsSmokeDumpShaderSources` завершился успешно на той же ветке PR #139: `result.json` показывает `status=passed`, 230400 non-black pixels, 8278 active marker pixels и пустое `error`; Gradle завершился `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.
- Дамп реально выбранного Complementary `shadow_entities.vsh` показывает, что после pack main выполняется `gl_Position.z = (gl_Position.z + gl_Position.w) * 0.5;`. Pack перед этим записывает `gl_Position = shadowProjection * shadowModelView * position` и масштабирует z на `0.2`; значит Vulkan depth remap находится после pack entrypoint и участвует в активной entity shadow программе. Этот факт сам по себе не подтверждает корректное положение естественной тени и не устраняет найденный Iris mismatch.

### Shadow texture coordinates and comparison reference — 2026-10-26

- Found the missing natural entity-shadow cause: VulkanMod records a negative-height viewport, while shader-pack shadow vertices retained OpenGL clip Y. The produced image was vertically mirrored relative to the UVs used by pack receivers. Terrain/entity pack wrappers now invert clip Y after pack execution; the native fallback shadow projection performs the same conversion. Shadow rasterization compensates face winding so the coordinate conversion preserves facing semantics.
- Removed the adapter-added 0.0005 comparison-reference bias from terrain, entity, textured and post paths. The shader pack supplies its own depth reference; emulated GL_LEQUAL now compares that reference directly. Removing bias alone did not fix the cast shadow; the Y conversion did.
- Full JDK 21 gate passed: 600 tests, 104 example shaders, 122 Vulkan SPIR-V modules, build, GraphicsSmoke compilation and example packaging. RTX 3070 Complementary Reimagined HIGH passed with 230,400 non-black pixels / 142 color buckets and no reported errors. In the matched golem-shadow ROI, mean luminance is 112.68 versus Iris 112.04; MAE fell from 22.67 to 5.33, and pixels where Iris is darker by >20 fell from 3,746 to 61. The long cast shadow is visible. This is generated-scene evidence, not full natural-world parity.
- Strengthened `shadow-color-entity` to require both shadow-color aliases and near depth in its expected OpenGL UV quadrant. On unmodified PR #140 it failed (active marker=0, disabled=0); on this candidate it passed (active=8,788, disabled=0).
- Pinned source audit passed: four packs, three dimensions, 12 defaults / 63 profile cases; 939 geometry fragments accepted, none rejected; all 18 active compute stages compiled.
- PR #140's cancelled Graphics job was retried successfully. Build, source audit and Graphics runs all passed; there were no review comments. Merged with expected head `4f8d01f`, producing main `9a1638e`. This candidate was fast-forwarded onto that main without changing its source tree. Further pack lifecycle checks and hosted candidate checks remain before release.
